TalkBack and fractional-ZMW actual speech testing must close before the next research pass (§438 D1); this replaces the research-only non-blocking treatment for that next pass only. §409 and accepted navigation limitations remain. SEC-DEPS-002 remains independently open solely for conditional 27 Oct 2026 D3 and governed closeout under unchanged §6f.27/§427/§428/§433. Its source merge, separate independently verified sole-JSON permanent-main §364 rebind and fetched-main RAP PASS precede the existing Implementer resuming. BRIDGE-MCP-001 is formally closed and retired by §438 D2. QOL-BUNDLE-001 is closed under §432; §6f.28 expired. CI-RUNNER-PIN-001 is closed under §426; §6f.26 expired. SEC-DEPS-001 is closed under §418; CODE-REVIEW-FIN-001 delivered under §419; §6f.23/§6f.24/§6f.25 expired. The observability.md:30 dev:docker mention remains a separate unauthorised follow-up. RESEARCH-RELEASE-001's §437 posture, both parents CLASS-A-VAL-002 and SE-REASON-001, SME held and the §409 limits remain. Steward records the effective SHA and stops under §363 without implementation.
